  %reinterpret_cast_1 = memref.reinterpret_cast %arg0 to offset: [%2], sizes: [1024], strides: [1] : memref<*xf32> to memref<1024xf32, strided<[1], offset: ?>>

  %7 = arith.subi %6, %2 : index

  cf.cond_br %10, ^bb2, ^bb3

  %15 = arith.cmpi sgt, %7, %12 : index
  %16 = arith.select %15, %14, %c0 : index
  %17 = vector.create_mask %16 : vector<4xi1>
  %18 = vector.maskedload %reinterpret_cast_1[%12], %17, %cst : memref<1024xf32, strided<[1], offset: ?>>, vector<4xi1>, vector<4xf32> into vector<4xf32>

  return
